## Headroom: which tasks can tell the arms apart

**149 of 150 tasks can tell the arms apart.** 0 passed in every arm on every try and 1 failed in every arm on every try. Those 1 can't show a difference between arms, whatever the arms do.

A "no worse than" or "better than" call needs at least 10 of these tasks among the two arms' paired tasks, and room on them for the smallest difference the preregistration named. Otherwise the call reads "can't tell: no room to improve".

Pass rate per arm, all scored tries: `cc-opus-none-twin` 52/60 (86.7%); `cc-opus-rules-twin` 58/60 (96.7%); `cc-opus-none-company` 37/60 (61.7%); `cc-opus-none-personal` 55/60 (91.7%); `cc-opus-open-company` 7/60 (11.7%); `cc-opus-open-personal` 40/60 (66.7%); `cc-opus-rules-company` 46/60 (76.7%); `cc-opus-rules-personal` 55/60 (91.7%); `codex-sol-none-twin` 0/60 (0%); `codex-sol-rules-twin` 54/60 (90%); `codex-sol-none-company` 45/60 (75%); `codex-sol-none-personal` 60/60 (100%); `codex-sol-open-company` 28/60 (46.7%); `codex-sol-open-personal` 59/60 (98.3%); `codex-sol-rules-company` 49/60 (81.7%); `codex-sol-rules-personal` 58/60 (96.7%); `hermes-sol-none-twin` 0/60 (0%); `hermes-sol-rules-twin` 55/60 (91.7%); `hermes-sol-none-company` 40/60 (66.7%); `hermes-sol-none-personal` 60/60 (100%); `hermes-sol-open-company` 10/60 (16.7%); `hermes-sol-open-personal` 34/60 (56.7%); `hermes-sol-rules-company` 43/60 (71.7%); `hermes-sol-rules-personal` 60/60 (100%); `hermes-particle-none-twin` 36/60 (60%); `hermes-particle-rules-twin` 52/59 (88.1%); `hermes-particle-none-company` 23/60 (38.3%); `hermes-particle-none-personal` 55/60 (91.7%); `hermes-particle-open-company` 7/60 (11.7%); `hermes-particle-open-personal` 15/60 (25%); `hermes-particle-rules-company` 36/60 (60%); `hermes-particle-rules-personal` 55/60 (91.7%); `oc-opus-none-twin` 0/60 (0%); `oc-opus-rules-twin` 56/60 (93.3%); `oc-opus-none-company` 39/60 (65%); `oc-opus-none-personal` 56/60 (93.3%); `oc-opus-open-company` 4/60 (6.7%); `oc-opus-open-personal` 27/60 (45%); `oc-opus-rules-company` 49/60 (81.7%); `oc-opus-rules-personal` 57/60 (95%); `dsh-sol-none-twin` 0/60 (0%); `dsh-sol-rules-twin` 59/60 (98.3%); `dsh-sol-none-company` 44/60 (73.3%); `dsh-sol-none-personal` 60/60 (100%); `dsh-sol-open-company` 31/60 (51.7%); `dsh-sol-open-personal` 59/60 (98.3%); `dsh-sol-rules-company` 53/60 (88.3%); `dsh-sol-rules-personal` 59/60 (98.3%).
